// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/logic/ResonanceStabilizationRituals.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.logic;

import com.example.arcanebridge.client.ResonanceVfxHandler;
import com.example.arcanebridge.network.ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket;
import com.example.arcanebridge.network.NetworkHandler;
import com.example.arcanebridge.registry.ModEffects;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.world.InteractionHand;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.Blocks;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.entity.BlockEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.level.block.state.BlockState;
import net.minecraftforge.event.TickEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.player.PlayerInteractEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
import net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = "arcane_bridge")
public class ResonanceStabilizationRituals {

    // =========================================================================
    // 💎 1. РИТУАЛ МЕДИТАЦИИ В АМЕТИСТОВОЙ ЖЕОДЕ («КРИСТАЛЛИЧЕСКОЕ НАСЫЩЕНИЕ»)
    // =========================================================================
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onPlayerTick(TickEvent.PlayerTickEvent event) {
        if (event.phase != TickEvent.Phase.END || event.player.level().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!(event.player instanceof ServerPlayer player)) return;

        CompoundTag pData = player.getPersistentData();
        float stability = pData.getFloat("ArcaneStability");

        // 1. Медитация в аметистовой жеоде (присед рядом с аметистовыми кластерами)
        if (stability <= 50.0f && player.isCrouching()) {
            handleGeodeMeditation(player);
        } else {
            pData.putInt("geode_meditate_ticks", 0);
        }

        // 2. Механический оверклокинг при удержании Shift и таймер удержания заряда (10 секунд)
        boolean isMech = pData.getBoolean("ArcaneMechOverload") || pData.getFloat("mech_caster_load") > 0;
        int mechChargeTicks = pData.getInt("mech_charge_ticks");
        boolean hasOverclock = player.hasEffect(ModEffects.KINETIC_OVERCLOCK.get());

        if (stability <= 50.0f && isMech && player.isCrouching()) {
            handleMechCrouchCharging(player);
        } else if (mechChargeTicks >= 25 && !hasOverclock) {
            // Игрок отпустил шифт — тикают 10 секунд (200 тиков) удержания
            int holdTicks = pData.getInt("mech_hold_ticks") - 1;
            pData.putInt("mech_hold_ticks", holdTicks);
            if (holdTicks <= 0) {
                // 10 секунд истекли — механический заряд сбрасывается!
                pData.putInt("mech_charge_ticks", 0);
                pData.putLong("mech_overclock_cooldown", player.level().getGameTime() + 100); // 5 сек кд после сброса
                pData.putInt("mech_max_cooldown", 100);
                player.serverLevel().playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.0, player.getZ(),
                        SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.7f, 1.3f);
                player.serverLevel().sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE,
                        player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.0, player.getZ(), 8, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.03);
            }
        }
    }

    private static void handleGeodeMeditation(ServerPlayer player) {
        CompoundTag pData = player.getPersistentData();
        int medTicks = pData.getInt("geode_meditate_ticks") + 1;
        pData.putInt("geode_meditate_ticks", medTicks);

        // Каждые 24 тика (~1.2 сек) щупальца сканируют и ломают 1 созревший кластер аметиста
        if (medTicks % 24 == 0) {
            ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();
            BlockPos playerPos = player.blockPosition();

            BlockPos foundCluster = null;
            double nearestDistSq = Double.MAX_VALUE;

            for (BlockPos target : BlockPos.betweenClosed(playerPos.offset(-4, -2, -4), playerPos.offset(4, 3, 4))) {
                BlockState state = level.getBlockState(target);
                if (state.is(Blocks.AMETHYST_CLUSTER)) {
                    double distSq = target.distSqr(playerPos);
                    if (distSq < nearestDistSq) {
                        nearestDistSq = distSq;
                        foundCluster = target.immutable();
                    }
                }
            }

            if (foundCluster != null) {
                // Щупальце протягивается и разбивает кластер
                double cx = foundCluster.getX() + 0.5;
                double cy = foundCluster.getY() + 0.5;
                double cz = foundCluster.getZ() + 0.5;

                NetworkHandler.sendToTrackingAndSelf(
                        new ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket(player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), cx, cy, cz, 1, false),
                        player
                );

                level.destroyBlock(foundCluster, false); // Ломаем кластер без ванильного выпадения (поглощен!)
                level.playSound(null, cx, cy, cz, SoundEvents.AMETHYST_CLUSTER_BREAK, SoundSource.BLOCKS, 0.9f, 1.3f);
                level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_CHIME, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.7f, 1.6f);

                int eaten = pData.getInt("amethyst_clusters_eaten") + 1;
                pData.putInt("amethyst_clusters_eaten", eaten);

                ItemStack echoShard = findItem(player, "echo_shard");
                boolean hasEchoShard = !echoShard.isEmpty();

                if (eaten >= 35 && hasEchoShard) {
                    // Тир 3: 45 минут (35 кластеров + Осколок эха) -> перк «Разрыв Пустоты»
                    echoShard.shrink(1);
                    player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.CRYSTALLINE_SATIATION.get(), 45 * 60 * 20, 2, false, true, true));
                    level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), SoundEvents.WARDEN_SONIC_BOOM, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.85f, 1.4f);
                    level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), SoundEvents.RESPAWN_ANCHOR_CHARGE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.2f);
                    level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SONIC_BOOM, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), 1, 0, 0, 0, 0);
                    pData.putInt("amethyst_clusters_eaten", 0);
                } else if (eaten >= 20) {
                    if (!hasEchoShard) {
                        // Тир 2: 25 минут (20 кластеров) -> +30% урона щупалец
                        player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.CRYSTALLINE_SATIATION.get(), 25 * 60 * 20, 1, false, true, true));
                        level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), SoundEvents.RESPAWN_ANCHOR_CHARGE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.9f, 1.4f);
                        pData.putInt("amethyst_clusters_eaten", 0);
                    } else {
                        // Есть Осколок эха — даем Тир 2 и продолжаем впитывать до 35!
                        player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.CRYSTALLINE_SATIATION.get(), 25 * 60 * 20, 1, false, true, true));
                        level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), SoundEvents.RESPAWN_ANCHOR_CHARGE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.7f, 1.6f);
                    }
                } else if (eaten == 10) {
                    // Тир 1: 15 минут (10 кластеров) -> базовая стабилизация
                    player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.CRYSTALLINE_SATIATION.get(), 15 * 60 * 20, 0, false, true, true));
                    level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.2, player.getZ(), SoundEvents.AMETHYST_CLUSTER_HIT, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.5f);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // ⚙️ 3. РИТУАЛ КАЛИБРОВКИ МЕХАНИЗМОВ («КАЛИБРОВАННЫЕ СЕРВОПРИВОДЫ»)
    // =========================================================================
    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onWrenchCalibration(PlayerInteractEvent.RightClickItem event) {
        Player player = event.getEntity();
        if (player.level().isClientSide || !(player instanceof ServerPlayer serverPlayer)) return;

        ItemStack held = event.getItemStack();
        ResourceLocation rl = ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(held.getItem());
        if (rl == null || !rl.getPath().contains("wrench")) return;

        if (!player.isCrouching()) return;

        CompoundTag pData = player.getPersistentData();
        float stability = pData.getFloat("ArcaneStability");
        boolean isMech = pData.getBoolean("ArcaneMechOverload") || pData.getFloat("mech_caster_load") > 0;
        if (stability > 70.0f && !isMech) return;

        int alloyCount = countItem(player, "andesite_alloy");
        int copperCount = countItem(player, "copper_ingot");
        int precCount = countItem(player, "precision_mechanism");

        int tier;
        int durationTicks;

        if (alloyCount >= 24 && precCount >= 1) {
            // Тир 3: 45 минут (24 сплава + Точный механизм) -> перк «Сброс Пара»
            tier = 2;
            durationTicks = 45 * 60 * 20;
            deductItem(player, "andesite_alloy", 24);
            deductItem(player, "precision_mechanism", 1);
        } else if (alloyCount >= 12 && copperCount >= 2) {
            // Тир 2: 25 минут (12 сплавов + 2 медных слитка) -> +30% к форсажу
            tier = 1;
            durationTicks = 25 * 60 * 20;
            deductItem(player, "andesite_alloy", 12);
            deductItem(player, "copper_ingot", 2);
        } else if (alloyCount >= 4) {
            // Тир 1: 15 минут (4 сплава) -> базовая калибровка
            tier = 0;
            durationTicks = 15 * 60 * 20;
            deductItem(player, "andesite_alloy", 4);
        } else {
            // Недостаточно ресурсов
            serverPlayer.serverLevel().playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.0, player.getZ(),
                    SoundEvents.ARMOR_EQUIP_IRON, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.6f, 0.6f);
            return;
        }

        // Применяем бафф калибровки соответствующего тира
        player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.CALIBRATED_SERVOS.get(), durationTicks, tier, false, true, true));

        // Звуки и визуал продувки сервоприводов
        ServerLevel level = serverPlayer.serverLevel();
        double px = player.getX();
        double py = player.getY() + 1.1;
        double pz = player.getZ();

        if (tier == 2) {
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.UI_TOAST_CHALLENGE_COMPLETE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 1.5f);
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.ANVIL_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.4f);
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.9f, 0.7f);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE, px, py, pz, 25, 0.4, 0.4, 0.4, 0.08);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ELECTRIC_SPARK, px, py, pz, 20, 0.5, 0.5, 0.5, 0.15);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, 10, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.05);
        } else if (tier == 1) {
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.ANVIL_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.9f, 1.3f);
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 0.8f);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE, px, py, pz, 18, 0.35, 0.35, 0.35, 0.06);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ELECTRIC_SPARK, px, py, pz, 15, 0.4, 0.4, 0.4, 0.12);
        } else {
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.ANVIL_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, 1.1f);
            level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.7f, 0.9f);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE, px, py, pz, 12, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.05);
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ELECTRIC_SPARK, px, py, pz, 10, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.08);
        }

        player.swing(event.getHand(), true);
    }

    public static int countItem(Player player, String pathSubstring) {
        int count = 0;
        for (ItemStack stack : player.getInventory().items) {
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                ResourceLocation rl = ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(stack.getItem());
                if (rl != null && rl.getPath().contains(pathSubstring)) {
                    count += stack.getCount();
                }
            }
        }
        return count;
    }

    public static ItemStack findItem(Player player, String pathSubstring) {
        for (ItemStack stack : player.getInventory().items) {
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                ResourceLocation rl = ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(stack.getItem());
                if (rl != null && rl.getPath().contains(pathSubstring)) {
                    return stack;
                }
            }
        }
        return ItemStack.EMPTY;
    }

    public static void deductItem(Player player, String pathSubstring, int amount) {
        int remaining = amount;
        for (ItemStack stack : player.getInventory().items) {
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                ResourceLocation rl = ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(stack.getItem());
                if (rl != null && rl.getPath().contains(pathSubstring)) {
                    int take = Math.min(stack.getCount(), remaining);
                    stack.shrink(take);
                    remaining -= take;
                    if (remaining <= 0) break;
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // =========================================================================
    // ⚡ 4. МЕХАНИЧЕСКИЙ ОВЕРКЛОКИНГ (КИСЛОТНЫЙ ФОРСАЖ СКОРОСТИ АТАКИ И ВЫСТРЕЛОВ)
    // =========================================================================
    private static void handleMechCrouchCharging(ServerPlayer player) {
        CompoundTag pData = player.getPersistentData();
        int chargeTicks = pData.getInt("mech_charge_ticks") + 1;
        pData.putInt("mech_charge_ticks", chargeTicks);

        long gameTime = player.level().getGameTime();
        long cdUntil = pData.getLong("mech_overclock_cooldown");
        if (gameTime < cdUntil) return;

        // Каждые 25 тиков приседа растет давление в сервоприводах
        if (chargeTicks % 25 == 0 && chargeTicks <= 100) {
            pData.putInt("mech_hold_ticks", 200); // 10 секунд удержания
            int bars = chargeTicks / 25;
            float pitch = 0.8f + (bars * 0.25f);
            player.serverLevel().playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.0, player.getZ(),
                    SoundEvents.ARMOR_EQUIP_IRON, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.8f, pitch);
            player.serverLevel().sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SMOKE, player.getX(), player.getY() + 0.8, player.getZ(), 8, 0.2, 0.2, 0.2, 0.02);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Активация форсажа при нанесении удара во время механического перегруза
     */
    public static void triggerKineticOverclock(ServerPlayer player) {
        CompoundTag pData = player.getPersistentData();
        int chargeTicks = pData.getInt("mech_charge_ticks");
        if (chargeTicks < 25) return;

        int bars = Math.min(4, chargeTicks / 25);
        pData.putInt("mech_charge_ticks", 0);
        pData.putInt("mech_hold_ticks", 0);

        long gameTime = player.level().getGameTime();

        // Длительность форсажа: ВСЕГДА ровно 5 секунд (100 тиков)!
        int duration = 100;

        // Кулдаун после спуска: 5 сек действия + bars * 5 сек отката (5с для тира 1, 10с для тира 2, 15с для тира 3, 20с для тира 4)
        int cdTicks = bars * 100;
        pData.putLong("mech_overclock_cooldown", gameTime + duration + cdTicks);
        pData.putInt("mech_max_cooldown", cdTicks);

        boolean hasMastery = pData.getBoolean("MatrixOverclockMastery");
        boolean hasCalibrated = player.hasEffect(ModEffects.CALIBRATED_SERVOS.get());
        int servoAmp = hasCalibrated ? player.getEffect(ModEffects.CALIBRATED_SERVOS.get()).getAmplifier() : -1;

        player.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(ModEffects.KINETIC_OVERCLOCK.get(), duration, bars - 1, false, true, true));

        // Перк Тир 3 «Сброс Пара»: мощный выхлоп под высоким давлением с фронтальным отбрасыванием
        if (servoAmp >= 2) {
            triggerSteamVentBurst(player, 8.0f);
        }

        // Начисление перегрузки (при эндгейм-матрицах = 0, при калиброванных сервоприводах - в 4 раза меньше!)
        float loadAdded = hasMastery ? 0.0f : ((bars * 0.5f) * (hasCalibrated ? 0.25f : 1.0f));

        if (loadAdded > 0.0f) {
            float curMech = pData.contains("mech_caster_load", 5) ? pData.getFloat("mech_caster_load") : (float) pData.getInt("mech_caster_load");
            pData.putFloat("mech_caster_load", curMech + loadAdded);
            pData.putInt("mech_shock_timer", 100);
        }

        ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();
        double px = player.getX();
        double py = player.getY() + 1.1;
        double pz = player.getZ();

        level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.FIRECHARGE_USE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.9f, 1.6f);
        level.playSound(null, px, py, pz, SoundEvents.ARMOR_EQUIP_NETHERITE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 1.2f);
        level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, 15, 0.3, 0.3, 0.3, 0.08);
    }

    /**
     * Перк «Сброс Пара» (Steam Vent Burst) — направленный выхлоп раскалённого пара под давлением
     */
    public static void triggerSteamVentBurst(ServerPlayer player, float damage) {
        ServerLevel level = player.serverLevel();
        net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 look = player.getLookAngle();
        net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 eyePos = player.getEyePosition();

        level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.0, player.getZ(),
                SoundEvents.GENERIC_EXPLODE, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 0.7f, 1.8f);
        level.playSound(null, player.getX(), player.getY() + 1.0, player.getZ(),
                SoundEvents.FIRE_EXTINGUISH, SoundSource.PLAYERS, 1.0f, 0.6f);

        // Конус пара перед игроком
        for (int i = 0; i < 22; i++) {
            double spread = 0.35;
            double px = eyePos.x + look.x * (1.0 + Math.random() * 3.5) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 3;
            double py = eyePos.y + look.y * (1.0 + Math.random() * 3.5) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 2;
            double pz = eyePos.z + look.z * (1.0 + Math.random() * 3.5) + (Math.random() - 0.5) * spread * 3;
            level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE, px, py, pz, 1, 0, 0.04, 0, 0.05);
            if (i % 2 == 0) {
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.FLAME, px, py, pz, 1, 0, 0.02, 0, 0.04);
            }
        }

        net.minecraft.world.phys.AABB coneBox = player.getBoundingBox().inflate(4.5);
        List<net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity> entities = level.getEntitiesOfClass(
                net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity.class, coneBox, e -> e != player && e.isAlive()
        );

        for (net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity target : entities) {
            net.minecraft.world.phys.Vec3 toTarget = target.position().subtract(player.position()).normalize();
            double dot = look.dot(toTarget);
            if (dot > 0.45) { // Конус ~65 градусов спереди
                target.hurt(player.damageSources().mobAttack(player), damage);
                target.setRemainingFireTicks(70);
                target.setDeltaMovement(toTarget.x * 0.95, 0.35, toTarget.z * 0.95);
                target.hurtMarked = true;
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/combat/ArchetypeDamageHandler.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.combat;

import com.example.arcanebridge.network.ClientboundSyncShieldPacket;
import com.example.arcanebridge.network.NetworkHandler;
import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.Tag;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundEvents;
import net.minecraft.sounds.SoundSource;
import net.minecraft.tags.DamageTypeTags;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageTypes;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffectInstance;
import net.minecraft.world.effect.MobEffects;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.AbstractArrow;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.projectile.Projectile;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.player.PlayerEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingHurtEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.EventPriority;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
import net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = MobArchetypes.MODID)
public class ArchetypeDamageHandler {

    @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGH)
    public static void onLivingHurt(LivingHurtEvent event) {
        LivingEntity target = event.getEntity();
        if (target.level().isClientSide() || target instanceof Player || !target.isAlive()) return;

        CompoundTag data = target.getPersistentData();
        if (data.getBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN)) return;
        if (!data.contains(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, Tag.TAG_LIST)) return;

        ListTag layers = data.getList(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
        int currentIndex = data.getInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX);
        if (layers.isEmpty() || currentIndex >= layers.size()) {
            data.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN, true);
            syncShieldToTracking(target);
            return;
        }

        float incomingDamage = event.getAmount();
        if (incomingDamage <= 0.0F) return;

        CompoundTag activeLayer = layers.getCompound(currentIndex);
        MobArchetypes.Type archetype;
        try {
            archetype = MobArchetypes.Type.valueOf(activeLayer.getString("Type"));
        } catch (Exception e) {
            return;
        }

        DamageSource source = event.getSource();
        MobArchetypes.AttackCategory category = classifyAttack(target, source);
        boolean isMatchingKey = isProfileMatch(archetype, category);

        // Профильное оружие наносит 100% урона по щиту, непрофильное штрафуется резистом (20%)
        float effectiveShieldDamage = isMatchingKey ? incomingDamage : (incomingDamage * MobArchetypes.SHIELD_DAMAGE_REDUCTION);

        ServerLevel level = (ServerLevel) target.level();
        float damageToProcess = effectiveShieldDamage;
        float totalAbsorbedByShield = 0.0F;

        // Поглощение урона текущим и последующими слоями
        while (damageToProcess > 0.0F && currentIndex < layers.size()) {
            CompoundTag currentLayer = layers.getCompound(currentIndex);
            float layerHp = currentLayer.getFloat("HP");

            if (damageToProcess <= layerHp) {
                currentLayer.putFloat("HP", layerHp - damageToProcess);
                totalAbsorbedByShield += damageToProcess;
                damageToProcess = 0.0F;
                triggerShieldHitEffects(level, target, archetype, isMatchingKey);
            } else {
                totalAbsorbedByShield += layerHp;
                damageToProcess -= layerHp;
                currentLayer.putFloat("HP", 0.0F);

                triggerShieldBreakEffects(level, target, archetype);
                currentIndex++;

                if (currentIndex < layers.size()) {
                    // Звук переключения на внутренний слой
                    level.playSound(null, target.getX(), target.getY(), target.getZ(),
                            SoundEvents.BEACON_POWER_SELECT, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 0.9F, 1.3F);
                    try {
                        archetype = MobArchetypes.Type.valueOf(layers.getCompound(currentIndex).getString("Type"));
                    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                }
            }
        }

        // Проверка полного снятия защиты
        if (currentIndex >= layers.size()) {
            data.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN, true);
            data.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_BROKEN, true);
            level.playSound(null, target.getX(), target.getY(), target.getZ(),
                    SoundEvents.GLASS_BREAK, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 1.3F, 0.85F);
        }

        data.putInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX, currentIndex);

        // 20% урона пробивает сквозь щит в здоровье моба + остаточный урон после полного снятия щитов
        float finalBodyDamage = (totalAbsorbedByShield * 0.20F) + damageToProcess;
        event.setAmount(finalBodyDamage);

        // Мгновенная синхронизация полосы со всеми клиентами
        syncShieldToTracking(target);
    }

    public static void syncShieldToTracking(LivingEntity entity) {
        CompoundTag data = entity.getPersistentData();
        CompoundTag syncTag = new CompoundTag();
        if (data.contains(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS)) {
            syncTag.put(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, data.getList(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, Tag.TAG_COMPOUND));
        }
        syncTag.putInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX, data.getInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX));
        syncTag.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN, data.getBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN));

        NetworkHandler.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.TRACKING_ENTITY_AND_SELF.with(() -> entity),
                new ClientboundSyncShieldPacket(entity.getId(), syncTag));
    }

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onStartTracking(PlayerEvent.StartTracking event) {
        if (event.getTarget() instanceof LivingEntity living && event.getEntity() instanceof ServerPlayer player) {
            CompoundTag data = living.getPersistentData();
            if (data.contains(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS)) {
                CompoundTag syncTag = new CompoundTag();
                syncTag.put(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, data.getList(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, Tag.TAG_COMPOUND));
                syncTag.putInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX, data.getInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX));
                syncTag.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN, data.getBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN));
                NetworkHandler.CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.PLAYER.with(() -> player),
                        new ClientboundSyncShieldPacket(living.getId(), syncTag));
            }
        }
    }

    private static MobArchetypes.AttackCategory classifyAttack(LivingEntity target, DamageSource source) {
        String damageTypeId = "";
        try {
            ResourceLocation key = target.level().registryAccess()
                    .registryOrThrow(Registries.DAMAGE_TYPE)
                    .getKey(source.type());
            if (key != null) damageTypeId = key.toString();
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        String msgId = source.getMsgId();
        Entity directEntity = source.getDirectEntity();

        if (damageTypeId.contains("ars_nouveau") || damageTypeId.contains("hexcasting") || damageTypeId.contains("magic")
                || source.is(DamageTypes.MAGIC) || source.is(DamageTypes.INDIRECT_MAGIC) || source.is(DamageTypeTags.WITCH_RESISTANT_TO)) {
            return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.MAGIC_SPELL;
        }
        if (directEntity != null) {
            ResourceLocation entId = ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(directEntity.getType());
            if (entId != null && (entId.getNamespace().equals("ars_nouveau") || entId.getNamespace().equals("hexcasting"))) {
                return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.MAGIC_SPELL;
            }
        }

        if (damageTypeId.contains("ntgl") || damageTypeId.contains("cgs") || damageTypeId.contains("createbigcannons")
                || damageTypeId.contains("machine_gun") || damageTypeId.contains("cannon") || damageTypeId.contains("bullet")
                || msgId.contains("bullet") || msgId.contains("cgs") || msgId.contains("cannon") || msgId.contains("machine_gun_fire")) {
            return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.CGS_FIREARM;
        }
        if (directEntity instanceof Projectile && !(directEntity instanceof AbstractArrow)) {
            ResourceLocation projId = ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(directEntity.getType());
            if (projId != null && (projId.getNamespace().equals("cgs") || projId.getNamespace().equals("ntgl")
                    || projId.getNamespace().equals("createbigcannons") || projId.getPath().contains("bullet")
                    || projId.getPath().contains("rocket") || projId.getPath().contains("projectile"))) {
                return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.CGS_FIREARM;
            }
        }

        if (directEntity instanceof AbstractArrow || damageTypeId.contains("arrow")) {
            return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.MELEE_STRIKE;
        }
        if (damageTypeId.equals("minecraft:player_attack") || damageTypeId.equals("minecraft:mob_attack")
                || (msgId.equals("player") && !damageTypeId.contains("spell") && !damageTypeId.contains("magic"))) {
            return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.MELEE_STRIKE;
        }

        return MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.GENERIC;
    }

    private static boolean isProfileMatch(MobArchetypes.Type archetype, MobArchetypes.AttackCategory category) {
        return switch (archetype) {
            case ARMORED -> category == MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.CGS_FIREARM;
            case ETHEREAL -> category == MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.MAGIC_SPELL;
            case BIO -> category == MobArchetypes.AttackCategory.MELEE_STRIKE;
            default -> false;
        };
    }

    private static void triggerShieldHitEffects(ServerLevel level, LivingEntity target, MobArchetypes.Type archetype, boolean isProfileHit) {
        double x = target.getX();
        double y = target.getY() + (target.getBbHeight() * 0.5D);
        double z = target.getZ();

        switch (archetype) {
            case ARMORED -> {
                level.playSound(null, x, y, z, SoundEvents.ANVIL_PLACE, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 0.6F, isProfileHit ? 1.5F : 0.8F);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ELECTRIC_SPARK, x, y, z, isProfileHit ? 8 : 3, 0.2D, 0.2D, 0.2D, 0.05D);
            }
            case ETHEREAL -> {
                level.playSound(null, x, y, z, SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_HIT, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 0.7F, isProfileHit ? 1.4F : 0.7F);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.WITCH, x, y, z, isProfileHit ? 10 : 3, 0.2D, 0.2D, 0.2D, 0.02D);
            }
            case BIO -> {
                level.playSound(null, x, y, z, SoundEvents.SLIME_BLOCK_HIT, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 0.7F, isProfileHit ? 1.2F : 0.6F);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SPORE_BLOSSOM_AIR, x, y, z, isProfileHit ? 12 : 4, 0.3D, 0.3D, 0.3D, 0.01D);
            }
            default -> {}
        }
    }

    private static void triggerShieldBreakEffects(ServerLevel level, LivingEntity target, MobArchetypes.Type archetype) {
        double x = target.getX();
        double y = target.getY() + (target.getBbHeight() * 0.5D);
        double z = target.getZ();

        switch (archetype) {
            case ARMORED -> {
                level.playSound(null, x, y, z, SoundEvents.ANVIL_DESTROY, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 1.0F, 1.1F);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CRIT, x, y, z, 20, 0.4D, 0.4D, 0.4D, 0.15D);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SMOKE, x, y, z, 15, 0.3D, 0.4D, 0.3D, 0.05D);
            }
            case ETHEREAL -> {
                level.playSound(null, x, y, z, SoundEvents.AMETHYST_BLOCK_BREAK, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 1.2F, 0.9F);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.REVERSE_PORTAL, x, y, z, 25, 0.4D, 0.5D, 0.4D, 0.1D);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.ENCHANTED_HIT, x, y, z, 15, 0.3D, 0.3D, 0.3D, 0.1D);
            }
            case BIO -> {
                level.playSound(null, x, y, z, SoundEvents.ZOMBIE_BREAK_WOODEN_DOOR, SoundSource.HOSTILE, 0.9F, 1.3F);
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.CAMPFIRE_COSY_SMOKE, x, y, z, 15, 0.4D, 0.4D, 0.4D, 0.05D);
                target.removeEffect(MobEffects.REGENERATION);
                target.addEffect(new MobEffectInstance(MobEffects.MOVEMENT_SLOWDOWN, 25, 2, false, false));
            }
            default -> {}
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/combat/BioMutantAuraTicker.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.combat;

import net.minecraft.core.particles.ParticleTypes;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerLevel;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = MobArchetypes.MODID)
public class BioMutantAuraTicker {

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onLivingTick(LivingEvent.LivingTickEvent event) {
        LivingEntity entity = event.getEntity();
        if (entity.level().isClientSide() || entity instanceof Player) return;
        if (entity.tickCount % 4 != 0) return; // Тикаем раз в 4 такта для оптимизации TPS

        CompoundTag data = entity.getPersistentData();
        if (!data.contains(MobArchetypes.NBT_ARCHETYPE)) return;

                // Проверяем, активен ли в данный момент слой био-мутанта
        if (data.getBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN)) return;

        net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag layers = data.getList(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, net.minecraft.nbt.Tag.TAG_COMPOUND);
        int currentIndex = data.getInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX);

        boolean hasActiveBioLayer = false;
        if (!layers.isEmpty() && currentIndex < layers.size()) {
            net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag activeLayer = layers.getCompound(currentIndex);
            if (MobArchetypes.Type.BIO.name().equals(activeLayer.getString("Type")) && activeLayer.getFloat("HP") > 0) {
                hasActiveBioLayer = true;
            }
        }

        if (hasActiveBioLayer) {

            ServerLevel level = (ServerLevel) entity.level();
            double time = entity.tickCount * 0.15;
            double radius = entity.getBbWidth() + 0.4;
            double yOffset = entity.getBbHeight() * 0.45;

            // Спавн 2 точек орбитального вихря спор
            for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
                double angle = time + (i * Math.PI);
                double px = entity.getX() + Math.cos(angle) * radius;
                double pz = entity.getZ() + Math.sin(angle) * radius;
                level.sendParticles(ParticleTypes.SPORE_BLOSSOM_AIR, px, entity.getY() + yOffset, pz, 1, 0.0, 0.02, 0.0, 0.0);
            }
        }
    }
}

================================================================================
// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/combat/DamageDebugLogger.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.combat;

import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.network.chat.Component;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraft.world.damagesource.DamageSource;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.Entity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Items;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.living.LivingHurtEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.EventPriority;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;
import net.minecraftforge.registries.ForgeRegistries;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = MobArchetypes.MODID)
public class DamageDebugLogger {

        @SubscribeEvent(priority = EventPriority.HIGHEST)
    public static void onEntityHurtDebug(LivingHurtEvent event) {
        LivingEntity target = event.getEntity();
        if (target.level().isClientSide()) return;

        // Ищем игроков с командным блоком в хотбаре в радиусе 32 блоков
        java.util.List<ServerPlayer> debugWatchers = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
        for (ServerPlayer player : target.level().getServer().getPlayerList().getPlayers()) {
            if (player.distanceToSqr(target) <= 1024 && hasCommandBlockInHotbar(player)) {
                debugWatchers.add(player);
            }
        }

        // Если ни у кого рядом нет командного блока в хотбаре — полностью глушим дебаг
        if (debugWatchers.isEmpty()) return;

        DamageSource source = event.getSource();
        Entity trueSource = source.getEntity();
        Entity directEntity = source.getDirectEntity();

        // 1. Получаем точный DamageType ID из реестра 1.20.1
        String damageTypeId = "unknown";
        try {
            ResourceLocation key = target.level().registryAccess()
                    .registryOrThrow(Registries.DAMAGE_TYPE)
                    .getKey(source.type());
            if (key != null) {
                damageTypeId = key.toString();
            }
        } catch (Exception ignored) {}

        // 2. Определяем сущность снаряда/атаки (Direct Entity)
        String directEntityName = "None (Direct)";
        if (directEntity != null) {
            ResourceLocation typeKey = ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(directEntity.getType());
            directEntityName = directEntity.getClass().getSimpleName() + " [" + (typeKey != null ? typeKey : "unknown") + "]";
        }

        // 3. Определяем инициатора урона (True Source: игрок, моб, пушка)
        String trueSourceName = "Environment / Block";
        String itemInHand = "N/A";
        if (trueSource != null) {
            ResourceLocation typeKey = ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(trueSource.getType());
            trueSourceName = trueSource.getClass().getSimpleName() + " [" + (typeKey != null ? typeKey : "unknown") + "]";
            if (trueSource instanceof LivingEntity livingSource) {
                ItemStack held = livingSource.getMainHandItem();
                itemInHand = held.isEmpty() ? "Empty Hand" : ForgeRegistries.ITEMS.getKey(held.getItem()).toString();
            }
        }

        String targetName = ForgeRegistries.ENTITY_TYPES.getKey(target.getType()).toString();

        // 4. Формируем подробный вывод
        String logConsole = String.format(
                "[ARCANE-COMBAT-DEBUG] Target: '%s' (ID:%d) | DmgType: '%s' | MsgId: '%s' | Direct: '%s' | TrueSource: '%s' | Item: '%s' | Amount: %.2f",
                targetName, target.getId(), damageTypeId, source.getMsgId(), directEntityName, trueSourceName, itemInHand, event.getAmount()
        );
        System.out.println(logConsole);

                // 5. Отправляем в чат только тем игрокам, у которых есть командный блок в хотбаре
        Component chatMsg = Component.literal(
                "§6§l[DEBUG DMG] §f" + targetName + " §7<= §c" + damageTypeId +
                " §7| Msg: §e" + source.getMsgId() +
                " §7| Direct: §b" + directEntityName +
                " §7| Source: §a" + trueSourceName +
                " §7| Dmg: §d" + String.format(java.util.Locale.US, "%.1f", event.getAmount())
        );

        for (ServerPlayer player : debugWatchers) {
            player.sendSystemMessage(chatMsg);
        }
    }

    /**
     * Проверка наличия любого командного блока в слотах хотбара (0-8)
     */
    private static boolean hasCommandBlockInHotbar(ServerPlayer player) {
        for (int slot = 0; slot < 9; slot++) {
            ItemStack stack = player.getInventory().getItem(slot);
            if (!stack.isEmpty()) {
                Item item = stack.getItem();
                if (item == Items.COMMAND_BLOCK || item == Items.CHAIN_COMMAND_BLOCK || item == Items.REPEATING_COMMAND_BLOCK) {
                    return true;
                }
            }
        }
        return false;
    }
}

// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/combat/MobArchetypes.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.combat;

import net.minecraft.core.registries.Registries;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.tags.TagKey;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EntityType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;

public class MobArchetypes {
    public static final String MODID = "arcane_bridge";

    // Строковые теги для ручного добавления через /tag @e[...] add <tag> или /summon ... {Tags:[...]}
    public static final String TAG_ARMORED = "arcane_armored";
    public static final String TAG_ETHEREAL = "arcane_ethereal";
    public static final String TAG_BIO = "arcane_bio";

    // Опциональные JSON-теги типов сущностей (fallback)
    public static final TagKey<EntityType<?>> ARMORED_TAG = TagKey.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, new ResourceLocation("arcane", "armored_automaton"));
    public static final TagKey<EntityType<?>> ETHEREAL_TAG = TagKey.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, new ResourceLocation("arcane", "ethereal_entity"));
    public static final TagKey<EntityType<?>> BIO_TAG = TagKey.create(Registries.ENTITY_TYPE, new ResourceLocation("arcane", "bio_mutant"));

    // 3. NBT-ключи в persistentData моба (Стек многослойных щитов)

    public static final String NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS = "ArcaneShieldLayers";

    public static final String NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX = "ArcaneCurrentLayerIndex";

    public static final String NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN = "ArcaneAllShieldsBroken";


    // Устаревшие ключи для обратной совместимости

    public static final String NBT_ARCHETYPE = "ArcaneArchetype";

    public static final String NBT_SHIELD_HP = "ArcaneShieldHP";

    public static final String NBT_MAX_SHIELD_HP = "ArcaneMaxShieldHP";

    public static final String NBT_SHIELD_BROKEN = "ArcaneShieldBroken";


    // 4. Базовые значения емкости барьеров (Shield HP)

    public static final float HP_ARMORED_SHIELD = 12.0f; // ~1-2 выстрела Create Gunsmithing

    public static final float HP_ETHEREAL_SHIELD = 15.0f; // ~1 боевой каст Hex / Ars

    public static final float HP_BIO_SHIELD = 10.0f;      // ~2 акцентированных удара в ближнем бою

    // Множитель сопротивления при активном щите для непрофильного оружия (20% входящего урона)
    public static final float SHIELD_DAMAGE_REDUCTION = 0.20f;

    public enum Type {
        NONE,
        ARMORED,
        ETHEREAL,
        BIO
    }

    public enum AttackCategory {
        CGS_FIREARM,   // Огнестрел Create: Gunsmithing
        MAGIC_SPELL,   // Магия Hex Casting / Ars Nouveau
        MELEE_STRIKE,  // Ближний бой / Кулаки Cyberware / Мечи
        GENERIC        // Окружение, огонь, кактусы и прочее
    }

    /**
     * Быстрое разрешение архетипа сущности с приоритетом ручных тегов
     */
    public static Type resolveArchetype(LivingEntity entity) {
        if (entity.getTags().contains(TAG_ARMORED)) return Type.ARMORED;
        if (entity.getTags().contains(TAG_ETHEREAL)) return Type.ETHEREAL;
        if (entity.getTags().contains(TAG_BIO)) return Type.BIO;

        if (entity.getType().is(ARMORED_TAG)) return Type.ARMORED;
        if (entity.getType().is(ETHEREAL_TAG)) return Type.ETHEREAL;
        if (entity.getType().is(BIO_TAG)) return Type.BIO;

        return Type.NONE;
    }
}

================================================================================
// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/combat/MobShieldSpawnHandler.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.combat;

import net.minecraft.core.registries.BuiltInRegistries;
import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.nbt.ListTag;
import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.EquipmentSlot;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.LivingEntity;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.MobType;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.ambient.AmbientCreature;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.Animal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.animal.WaterAnimal;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.decoration.ArmorStand;
import net.minecraft.world.entity.player.Player;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorItem;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ArmorMaterials;
import net.minecraft.world.item.ItemStack;
import net.minecraft.world.level.Level;
import net.minecraftforge.event.entity.EntityJoinLevelEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.eventbus.api.SubscribeEvent;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.common.Mod;

import java.lang.reflect.Method;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Random;

@Mod.EventBusSubscriber(modid = MobArchetypes.MODID, bus = Mod.EventBusSubscriber.Bus.FORGE)
public class MobShieldSpawnHandler {

    private static final String NBT_INITIALIZED = "arcane_shield_initialized";
    private static final Random RNG = new Random();

    @SubscribeEvent
    public static void onEntityJoinLevel(EntityJoinLevelEvent event) {
        if (event.getLevel().isClientSide()) return;
        if (!(event.getEntity() instanceof LivingEntity target)) return;

        if (target instanceof Player || target instanceof ArmorStand) return;
        if (target instanceof Animal || target instanceof WaterAnimal || target instanceof AmbientCreature) return;

        CompoundTag persistentData = target.getPersistentData();
        if (persistentData.getBoolean(NBT_INITIALIZED)) return;
        persistentData.putBoolean(NBT_INITIALIZED, true);

        if (tryApplyCommandTags(target, persistentData)) return;

        float maxHp = target.getMaxHealth();
        // Ранг 0 (Рядовой / Fodder) — щиты не выдаются
        if (maxHp < 25.0F) return;

        Level level = event.getLevel();
        String stage = getMajruszStage(level);

        int layerCount = 0;

        // Определение ранга и количества слоев
        if (maxHp < 45.0F) {
            // Ветеран (25 - 44 HP)
            float chance = switch (stage) {
                case "MASTER" -> 0.60F;
                case "EXPERT" -> 0.35F;
                default -> 0.10F;
            };
            if (RNG.nextFloat() < chance) layerCount = 1;
        } else if (maxHp < 80.0F) {
            // Элита (45 - 79 HP)
            float chance = switch (stage) {
                case "MASTER" -> 1.00F;
                case "EXPERT" -> 0.80F;
                default -> 0.40F;
            };
            if (RNG.nextFloat() < chance) {
                if (stage.equals("NORMAL")) {
                    layerCount = 1;
                } else if (stage.equals("EXPERT")) {
                    layerCount = (RNG.nextFloat() < 0.25F) ? 2 : 1;
                } else {
                    layerCount = (RNG.nextFloat() < 0.50F) ? 2 : 1;
                }
            }
        } else if (maxHp <= 160.0F) {
            // Мини-босс / Чемпион (80 - 160 HP)
            layerCount = (stage.equals("MASTER") && RNG.nextFloat() < 0.30F) ? 3 : 2;
        } else {
            // Босс / Титан (> 160 HP)
            layerCount = 3;
        }

        if (layerCount <= 0) return;

        // Расчет базового щитового бюджета
        float baseBudget;
        if (maxHp < 100.0F) {
            baseBudget = maxHp * 0.32F;
        } else if (maxHp <= 300.0F) {
            baseBudget = maxHp * 0.36F;
        } else {
            baseBudget = maxHp * 0.40F; // Для 1000 HP базовый бюджет = 400 HP
        }

        // Дробление и масштабирование пула щита
        float layerHp;
        if (layerCount == 1) {
            layerHp = (float) Math.ceil(baseBudget);
        } else if (layerCount == 2) {
            float totalPool = baseBudget * 1.25F; // 500 при 1000 HP
            layerHp = (float) Math.ceil(totalPool / 2.0F); // по 250 HP
        } else {
            float totalPool = baseBudget * 1.50F; // 600 при 1000 HP
            layerHp = (float) Math.ceil(totalPool / 3.0F); // по 200 HP
        }

        // Сборка тактической цепочки уникальных слоев (без дублей)
        List<String> archetypes = buildUniqueArchetypeChain(target, layerCount);
        ListTag layersList = new ListTag();

        for (String arch : archetypes) {
            CompoundTag layer = new CompoundTag();
            layer.putString("Type", arch);
            layer.putFloat("HP", layerHp);
            layer.putFloat("MaxHP", layerHp);
            layersList.add(layer);
        }

                persistentData.put(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, layersList);
        persistentData.putInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX, 0);
        persistentData.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN, false);

        // Мгновенная синхронизация созданных щитов моба с клиентами
        ArchetypeDamageHandler.syncShieldToTracking(target);
    }

    /**
     * Генерация цепочки архетипов без дубликатов с правильным порядком слоев
     */
    private static List<String> buildUniqueArchetypeChain(LivingEntity entity, int count) {
        List<String> pool = new ArrayList<>();
        pool.add("ARMORED");
        pool.add("ETHEREAL");
        pool.add("BIO");

        String priority = getPriorityArchetype(entity);
        List<String> result = new ArrayList<>();

        if (count == 1) {
            result.add(priority);
            return result;
        }

        if (count == 2) {
            pool.remove(priority);
            Collections.shuffle(pool, RNG);
            String secondary = pool.get(0);

            // Тактический порядок: Механика глубже, Эфир/Био на поверхности
            if (priority.equals("ARMORED")) {
                result.add(secondary); // Внешний
                result.add(priority);  // Внутренний
            } else {
                result.add(priority);  // Внешний
                result.add(secondary); // Внутренний
            }
            return result;
        }

        // 3 слоя: присутствуют все три типа в тактическом порядке
        if (priority.equals("ARMORED")) {
            result.add("ETHEREAL"); // Внешний
            result.add("BIO");      // Средний
            result.add("ARMORED");  // Внутренний
        } else if (priority.equals("ETHEREAL")) {
            result.add("ETHEREAL");
            result.add("BIO");
            result.add("ARMORED");
        } else {
            result.add("BIO");
            result.add("ETHEREAL");
            result.add("ARMORED");
        }
        return result;
    }

    private static String getPriorityArchetype(LivingEntity entity) {
        if (hasHeavyArmor(entity)) return "ARMORED";
        if (isMagicOrAirEntity(entity)) return "ETHEREAL";
        if (isBioEntity(entity)) return "BIO";

        int roll = RNG.nextInt(3);
        return roll == 0 ? "ARMORED" : (roll == 1 ? "ETHEREAL" : "BIO");
    }

    private static boolean hasHeavyArmor(LivingEntity entity) {
        for (EquipmentSlot slot : EquipmentSlot.values()) {
            if (slot.getType() != EquipmentSlot.Type.ARMOR) continue;
            ItemStack stack = entity.getItemBySlot(slot);
            if (stack.getItem() instanceof ArmorItem armor) {
                var mat = armor.getMaterial();
                if (mat == ArmorMaterials.IRON || mat == ArmorMaterials.DIAMOND ||
                    mat == ArmorMaterials.NETHERITE || mat == ArmorMaterials.CHAIN) return true;
                String name = mat.getName().toLowerCase();
                if (name.contains("brass") || name.contains("steel") || name.contains("enderium")) return true;
            }
        }
        return false;
    }

    private static boolean isMagicOrAirEntity(LivingEntity entity) {
        ResourceLocation id = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(entity.getType());
        String path = id.getPath().toLowerCase();
        String namespace = id.getNamespace().toLowerCase();

        return namespace.equals("ars_nouveau") || namespace.equals("hexcasting") ||
               path.contains("witch") || path.contains("evoker") || path.contains("illusioner") ||
               path.contains("enderman") || path.contains("phantom") || path.contains("blaze") ||
               path.contains("ghast") || path.contains("shulker") || path.contains("stalker");
    }

    private static boolean isBioEntity(LivingEntity entity) {
        if (entity.getMobType() == MobType.ARTHROPOD) return true;
        ResourceLocation id = BuiltInRegistries.ENTITY_TYPE.getKey(entity.getType());
        String path = id.getPath().toLowerCase();

        return path.contains("husk") || path.contains("slime") || path.contains("spider") ||
               path.contains("silverfish") || path.contains("drowned") || path.contains("mutant");
    }

    private static String getMajruszStage(Level level) {
        try {
            Class<?> helperClass = Class.forName("com.majruszsdifficulty.gamestage.GameStageHelper");
            for (Method m : helperClass.getMethods()) {
                if (m.getParameterCount() == 0 && (m.getName().equals("getGameStage") || m.getName().equals("getCurrentStage"))) {
                    Object stageObj = m.invoke(null);
                    if (stageObj != null) return stageObj.toString().toUpperCase();
                }
            }
        } catch (Throwable ignored) {}

        if (level.dimension() == Level.END) return "MASTER";
        if (level.dimension() == Level.NETHER) return "EXPERT";
        return "NORMAL";
    }

    private static boolean tryApplyCommandTags(LivingEntity target, CompoundTag persistentData) {
        String manualType = null;
        if (target.getTags().contains("arcane_armored")) manualType = "ARMORED";
        else if (target.getTags().contains("arcane_ethereal")) manualType = "ETHEREAL";
        else if (target.getTags().contains("arcane_bio")) manualType = "BIO";

        if (manualType == null) return false;

        float hp = Math.max(12.0F, (float) Math.ceil(target.getMaxHealth() * 0.25F));
        ListTag list = new ListTag();
        CompoundTag layer = new CompoundTag();
        layer.putString("Type", manualType);
        layer.putFloat("HP", hp);
        layer.putFloat("MaxHP", hp);
        list.add(layer);

        persistentData.put(MobArchetypes.NBT_SHIELD_LAYERS, list);
        persistentData.putInt(MobArchetypes.NBT_CURRENT_LAYER_INDEX, 0);
        persistentData.putBoolean(MobArchetypes.NBT_ALL_SHIELDS_BROKEN, false);
        return true;
    }
}

// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/decipher/DecryptionRegistry.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.decipher;

import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.world.item.Item;

import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.Optional;

public class DecryptionRegistry {

        public record DecryptionEntry(
            Item sealedItem,
            Item decipheredItem,
            ResourceLocation advancementId,
            String successDialogueNode,
            String spellName
    ) {}

    private static final Map<Item, DecryptionEntry> REGISTRY = new HashMap<>();

    public static void register(Item sealedItem, Item decipheredItem, ResourceLocation advancementId, String successDialogueNode, String spellName) {
        REGISTRY.put(sealedItem, new DecryptionEntry(sealedItem, decipheredItem, advancementId, successDialogueNode, spellName));
    }

    public static Optional<DecryptionEntry> getEntry(Item item) {
        return Optional.ofNullable(REGISTRY.get(item));
    }

    public static Collection<DecryptionEntry> getAllEntries() {
        return REGISTRY.values();
    }
}

// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/network/ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.network;

import net.minecraft.nbt.CompoundTag;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraftforge.fluids.capability.templates.FluidTank;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.HashMap;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket {

    // Клиентский кэш для отображения в очках инженера и HUD
    public static final Map<Integer, FluidTank> CLIENT_CHANNELS = new HashMap<>();

    private final int channelId;
    private final CompoundTag tankTag;

    public ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket(int channelId, CompoundTag tankTag) {
        this.channelId = channelId;
        this.tankTag = tankTag;
    }

    public ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.channelId = buf.readVarInt();
        this.tankTag = buf.readNbt();
    }

    public void toBytes(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeVarInt(this.channelId);
        buf.writeNbt(this.tankTag);
    }

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> ctx) {
        ctx.get().enqueueWork(() -> {
            FluidTank clientTank = CLIENT_CHANNELS.computeIfAbsent(this.channelId, id -> new FluidTank(16000));
            if (this.tankTag != null) {
                clientTank.readFromNBT(this.tankTag);
            }
        });
        ctx.get().setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/network/ClientboundRaidVfxPacket.java

package com.example.arcanebridge.network;

import com.example.arcanebridge.client.RiftSkyRenderer;
import net.minecraft.core.BlockPos;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.DistExecutor;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;

import java.util.UUID;
import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class ClientboundRaidVfxPacket {

    private final boolean isPrepActive;
    private final int prepTimerSeconds;
    private final int maxPrepSeconds;
    private final boolean isRaidActive;
    private final String raidType;
    private final UUID targetPlayerUUID;
    private final BlockPos epicenter;
    private final ResourceLocation dimension;

    public ClientboundRaidVfxPacket(boolean isPrepActive, int prepTimerSeconds, int maxPrepSeconds,
                                   boolean isRaidActive, String raidType, UUID targetPlayerUUID, BlockPos epicenter,
                                   ResourceLocation dimension) {
        this.isPrepActive = isPrepActive;
        this.prepTimerSeconds = prepTimerSeconds;
        this.maxPrepSeconds = maxPrepSeconds;
        this.isRaidActive = isRaidActive;
        this.raidType = raidType != null ? raidType : "arcane_breach";
        this.targetPlayerUUID = targetPlayerUUID != null ? targetPlayerUUID : new UUID(0L, 0L);
        this.epicenter = epicenter != null ? epicenter : BlockPos.ZERO;
        this.dimension = dimension != null ? dimension : new ResourceLocation("minecraft", "overworld");
    }

    public ClientboundRaidVfxPacket(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.isPrepActive = buf.readBoolean();
        this.prepTimerSeconds = buf.readInt();
        this.maxPrepSeconds = buf.readInt();
        this.isRaidActive = buf.readBoolean();
        this.raidType = buf.readUtf(64);
        this.targetPlayerUUID = buf.readUUID();
        this.epicenter = buf.readBlockPos();
        this.dimension = buf.readResourceLocation();
    }

    public static void encode(ClientboundRaidVfxPacket msg, FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.isPrepActive);
        buf.writeInt(msg.prepTimerSeconds);
        buf.writeInt(msg.maxPrepSeconds);
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.isRaidActive);
        buf.writeUtf(msg.raidType);
        buf.writeUUID(msg.targetPlayerUUID);
        buf.writeBlockPos(msg.epicenter);
        buf.writeResourceLocation(msg.dimension);
    }

    public static ClientboundRaidVfxPacket decode(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        return new ClientboundRaidVfxPacket(buf);
    }

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> supplier) {
        NetworkEvent.Context context = supplier.get();
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            DistExecutor.unsafeRunWhenOn(Dist.CLIENT, () -> () -> {
                RiftSkyRenderer.updateRaidState(
                        this.isPrepActive,
                        this.prepTimerSeconds,
                        this.maxPrepSeconds,
                        this.isRaidActive,
                        this.raidType,
                        this.targetPlayerUUID,
                        this.epicenter,
                        this.dimension
                );
            });
        });
        context.setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.example.arcanebridge.network;

import java.util.function.Supplier;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

public class ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket {
    private final float stability;
    private final int mechLoad;
    private final int arcaneLoad;
    private final int eleLoad;
    private final int mechLimit;
    private final int arcaneLimit;
    private final int eleLimit;

    private final boolean mechShock;
    private final boolean arcaneShock;
    private final boolean eleShock;

    private final boolean inRaidZone;

    private final int tendrilStacks;
    private final int tendrilAwakeningTicks;
    private final int mechChargeBars;
    private final int tendrilCd;
    private final int tendrilMaxCd;
    private final int mechCd;
    private final int mechMaxCd;

    public static float clientStability = 100.0F;
    public static int mechLoadStatic = 0;
    public static int arcaneLoadStatic = 0;
    public static int eleLoadStatic = 0;
    public static int mechLimitStatic = 2;
    public static int arcaneLimitStatic = 2;
    public static int eleLimitStatic = 2;

    public static boolean mechShockStatic = false;
    public static boolean arcaneShockStatic = false;
    public static boolean eleShockStatic = false;

    // Статическая переменная для клиента
    public static boolean inRaidZoneStatic = false;

    // Диегетические боевые состояния для визуала без цифр
    public static int tendrilStacksStatic = 0;
    public static int tendrilAwakeningTicksStatic = 0;
    public static int mechChargeBarsStatic = 0;
    public static int tendrilCdStatic = 0;
    public static int tendrilMaxCdStatic = 100;
    public static int mechCdStatic = 0;
    public static int mechMaxCdStatic = 100;

    public ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket(float stability, int mechLoad, int arcaneLoad, int eleLoad,
                                          int mechLimit, int arcaneLimit, int eleLimit,
                                          boolean mechShock, boolean arcaneShock, boolean eleShock,
                                          boolean inRaidZone,
                                          int tendrilStacks, int tendrilAwakeningTicks, int mechChargeBars,
                                          int tendrilCd, int tendrilMaxCd, int mechCd, int mechMaxCd) {
        this.stability = stability;
        this.mechLoad = mechLoad;
        this.arcaneLoad = arcaneLoad;
        this.eleLoad = eleLoad;
        this.mechLimit = mechLimit;
        this.arcaneLimit = arcaneLimit;
        this.eleLimit = eleLimit;
        this.mechShock = mechShock;
        this.arcaneShock = arcaneShock;
        this.eleShock = eleShock;
        this.inRaidZone = inRaidZone;
        this.tendrilStacks = tendrilStacks;
        this.tendrilAwakeningTicks = tendrilAwakeningTicks;
        this.mechChargeBars = mechChargeBars;
        this.tendrilCd = tendrilCd;
        this.tendrilMaxCd = tendrilMaxCd;
        this.mechCd = mechCd;
        this.mechMaxCd = mechMaxCd;
    }

    public ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.stability = buf.readFloat();
        this.mechLoad = buf.readInt();
        this.arcaneLoad = buf.readInt();
        this.eleLoad = buf.readInt();
        this.mechLimit = buf.readInt();
        this.arcaneLimit = buf.readInt();
        this.eleLimit = buf.readInt();
        this.mechShock = buf.readBoolean();
        this.arcaneShock = buf.readBoolean();
        this.eleShock = buf.readBoolean();
        this.inRaidZone = buf.readBoolean();
        this.tendrilStacks = buf.readInt();
        this.tendrilAwakeningTicks = buf.readInt();
        this.mechChargeBars = buf.readInt();
        this.tendrilCd = buf.readInt();
        this.tendrilMaxCd = buf.readInt();
        this.mechCd = buf.readInt();
        this.mechMaxCd = buf.readInt();
    }

    public static void encode(ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket msg, FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeFloat(msg.stability);
        buf.writeInt(msg.mechLoad);
        buf.writeInt(msg.arcaneLoad);
        buf.writeInt(msg.eleLoad);
        buf.writeInt(msg.mechLimit);
        buf.writeInt(msg.arcaneLimit);
        buf.writeInt(msg.eleLimit);
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.mechShock);
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.arcaneShock);
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.eleShock);
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.inRaidZone);
        buf.writeInt(msg.tendrilStacks);
        buf.writeInt(msg.tendrilAwakeningTicks);
        buf.writeInt(msg.mechChargeBars);
        buf.writeInt(msg.tendrilCd);
        buf.writeInt(msg.tendrilMaxCd);
        buf.writeInt(msg.mechCd);
        buf.writeInt(msg.mechMaxCd);
    }

    public static ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket decode(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        return new ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket(buf);
    }

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> supplier) {
        NetworkEvent.Context context = supplier.get();
        context.enqueueWork(() -> {
            clientStability = this.stability;
            mechLoadStatic = this.mechLoad;
            arcaneLoadStatic = this.arcaneLoad;
            eleLoadStatic = this.eleLoad;
            mechLimitStatic = this.mechLimit;
            arcaneLimitStatic = this.arcaneLimit;
            eleLimitStatic = this.eleLimit;
            mechShockStatic = this.mechShock;
            arcaneShockStatic = this.arcaneShock;
            eleShockStatic = this.eleShock;

            // Обновляем флаг зоны рейда на клиенте
            inRaidZoneStatic = this.inRaidZone;

            // Диегетические состояния
            tendrilStacksStatic = this.tendrilStacks;
            tendrilAwakeningTicksStatic = this.tendrilAwakeningTicks;
            mechChargeBarsStatic = this.mechChargeBars;
            tendrilCdStatic = this.tendrilCd;
            tendrilMaxCdStatic = this.tendrilMaxCd;
            mechCdStatic = this.mechCd;
            mechMaxCdStatic = this.mechMaxCd;
        });
        context.setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.example.arcanebridge.network;

import com.example.arcanebridge.client.render.ShieldBarOverlayRenderer;

import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;

import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class ClientboundSyncShieldPacket {

    private final int entityId;
    private final CompoundTag shieldData;

    public ClientboundSyncShieldPacket(int entityId, CompoundTag shieldData) {
        this.entityId = entityId;
        this.shieldData = shieldData;
    }

    public static void encode(ClientboundSyncShieldPacket msg, FriendlyByteBuf buffer) {
        buffer.writeInt(msg.entityId);
        buffer.writeNbt(msg.shieldData);
    }

    public static ClientboundSyncShieldPacket decode(FriendlyByteBuf buffer) {
        return new ClientboundSyncShieldPacket(buffer.readInt(), buffer.readNbt());

    public static void handle(ClientboundSyncShieldPacket msg, Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> ctx) {
        ctx.get().enqueueWork(() -> DistExecutor.unsafeRunWhenOn(
                Dist.CLIENT, () -> () -> ShieldBarOverlayRenderer.handleSyncShieldPacket(msg.entityId, msg.shieldData)
        ));
        ctx.get().setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

package com.example.arcanebridge.network;

import com.example.arcanebridge.client.ResonanceVfxHandler;
import net.minecraft.network.FriendlyByteBuf;
import net.minecraftforge.api.distmarker.Dist;
import net.minecraftforge.fml.DistExecutor;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkEvent;

import java.util.function.Supplier;

public class ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket {
    private final double startX, startY, startZ;
    private final double endX, endY, endZ;
    private final int stacks;
    private final boolean isElemental;

    public ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket(double startX, double startY, double startZ,
                                         double endX, double endY, double endZ,
                                         int stacks, boolean isElemental) {
        this.startX = startX;
        this.startY = startY;
        this.startZ = startZ;
        this.endX = endX;
        this.endY = endY;
        this.endZ = endZ;
        this.stacks = stacks;
        this.isElemental = isElemental;
    }

    public ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        this.startX = buf.readDouble();
        this.startY = buf.readDouble();
        this.startZ = buf.readDouble();
        this.endX = buf.readDouble();
        this.endY = buf.readDouble();
        this.endZ = buf.readDouble();
        this.stacks = buf.readInt();
        this.isElemental = buf.readBoolean();
    }

    public static void encode(ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket msg, FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        buf.writeDouble(msg.startX);
        buf.writeDouble(msg.startY);
        buf.writeDouble(msg.startZ);
        buf.writeDouble(msg.endX);
        buf.writeDouble(msg.endY);
        buf.writeDouble(msg.endZ);
        buf.writeInt(msg.stacks);
        buf.writeBoolean(msg.isElemental);
    }

    public static ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket decode(FriendlyByteBuf buf) {
        return new ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket(buf);
    }

    public void handle(Supplier<NetworkEvent.Context> supplier) {
        NetworkEvent.Context context = supplier.get();
        context.enqueueWork(() -> DistExecutor.unsafeRunWhenOn(Dist.CLIENT, () -> () -> ResonanceVfxHandler.handleTendrilStrikePacket(
                this.startX, this.startY, this.startZ, this.endX, this.endY, this.endZ, this.stacks, this.isElemental)));
        context.setPacketHandled(true);
    }
}

// FILE: src/main/java/com/example/arcanebridge/network/NetworkHandler.java
================================================================================

package com.example.arcanebridge.network;

import net.minecraft.resources.ResourceLocation;
import net.minecraft.server.level.ServerPlayer;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkDirection;
import net.minecraftforge.network.NetworkRegistry;
import net.minecraftforge.network.PacketDistributor;
import net.minecraftforge.network.simple.SimpleChannel;

public class NetworkHandler {
    private static final String PROTOCOL_VERSION = "1";
    public static final SimpleChannel CHANNEL = NetworkRegistry.newSimpleChannel(
            new ResourceLocation("arcane_bridge", "main"),
            () -> PROTOCOL_VERSION,
            PROTOCOL_VERSION::equals,
            PROTOCOL_VERSION::equals
    );

    private static int packetId = 0;

    public static void register() {
        // 1. Синхронизация резонанса
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT)
                .encoder(ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket::encode)
                .decoder(ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket::decode)
                .consumerMainThread(ClientboundResonanceSyncPacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 2. Сбой управления WASD
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(PacketSyncControlGlitch.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT)
                .encoder(PacketSyncControlGlitch::toBytes)
                .decoder(PacketSyncControlGlitch::new)
                .consumerMainThread(PacketSyncControlGlitch::handle)
                .add();

        // 3. Визуальные эффекты рейдов
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ClientboundRaidVfxPacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT)
                .encoder(ClientboundRaidVfxPacket::encode)
                .decoder(ClientboundRaidVfxPacket::decode)
                .consumerMainThread(ClientboundRaidVfxPacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 4. Завершение рейда
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ServerboundRaidEndPacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_SERVER)
                .encoder(ServerboundRaidEndPacket::encode)
                .decoder(ServerboundRaidEndPacket::decode)
                .consumerMainThread(ServerboundRaidEndPacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 5. Завершение калибровки брони (GUI -> Сервер)
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ServerboundRepairCompletePacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_SERVER)
                .encoder(ServerboundRepairCompletePacket::encode)
                .decoder(ServerboundRepairCompletePacket::decode)
                .consumerMainThread(ServerboundRepairCompletePacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 6. Запрос действий Гида / Локатора (GUI -> Сервер)
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ServerboundGuideActionPacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_SERVER)
                .encoder(ServerboundGuideActionPacket::toBytes)
                .decoder(ServerboundGuideActionPacket::new)
                .consumerMainThread(ServerboundGuideActionPacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 7. Синхронизация фазовых резервуаров жидкости (Сервер -> Клиент)
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT)
                .encoder(ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket::toBytes)
                .decoder(ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket::new)
                .consumerMainThread(ClientboundPhaseFluidSyncPacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 8. Синхронизация NBT щитов мобов (Сервер -> Клиент)
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ClientboundSyncShieldPacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT)
                .encoder(ClientboundSyncShieldPacket::encode)
                .decoder(ClientboundSyncShieldPacket::decode)
                .consumerMainThread(ClientboundSyncShieldPacket::handle)
                .add();

        // 9. Визуализация направленного удара щупалец Ars Nouveau
        CHANNEL.messageBuilder(ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket.class, packetId++, NetworkDirection.PLAY_TO_CLIENT)
                .encoder(ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket::encode)
                .decoder(ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket::decode)
                .consumerMainThread(ClientboundTendrilStrikePacket::handle)
                .add();
    }

    public static <MSG> void sendToPlayer(MSG message, ServerPlayer player) {
        if (player != null) {
            CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.PLAYER.with(() -> player), message);
        }
    }

    public static <MSG> void sendToPlayer(ServerPlayer player, MSG message) {
        if (player != null) {
            CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.PLAYER.with(() -> player), message);
        }
    }

    public static <MSG> void sendToTrackingAndSelf(MSG message, ServerPlayer player) {
        if (player != null) {
            CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.TRACKING_ENTITY_AND_SELF.with(() -> player), message);
        }
    }

    public static <MSG> void sendToServer(MSG message) {
        CHANNEL.sendToServer(message);
    }

    public static <MSG> void sendToAll(MSG message) {
        CHANNEL.send(PacketDistributor.ALL.noArg(), message);
    }
}
